Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on sheaves structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 95. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 332 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the sheaves decomposition. By minimality, the element a^95 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 332-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
